- **voltagent-research**: Research, search, and analysis specialists - market research, competitive analysis, trend forecasting, and idea validation ([VoltAgent/awesome-claude-code-subagents](https://github.com/VoltAgent/awesome-claude-code-subagents/tree/HEAD/categories/10-research-analysis))
- **voltagent-research**: Research, search, and analysis specialists - market research, competitive analysis, trend forecasting ([fubotv/smo-subagents](https://github.com/fubotv/smo-subagents/tree/HEAD/categories/10-research-analysis))
- **voltagent-skills**: Skills for building AI applications with VoltAgent: project setup, best practices, core reference, and embedded docs lookup. ([VoltAgent/skills](https://github.com/VoltAgent/skills))
- **voltaire**: Revenue intelligence for SaaS paywall conversion. Voltaire connects Stripe data, installs behavioral tracking into the codebase, and runs three AI agents on every /voltaire: timing analysis, cohort comparison, and churn tracking to surface where conversion is being lost and what to fix. ([voltairehq/voltaire-plugin](https://github.com/voltairehq/voltaire-plugin))
- **voltaire-effect**: Progressive disclosure skills for voltaire-effect - Effect.ts integration for Voltaire Ethereum primitives with typed errors, dependency injection, providers, signers, and crypto ([cyotee/voltaire-skill](https://github.com/cyotee/voltaire-skill))
- **voltron-lions**: Turn one objective into a reviewed plan, then execute it with five parallel agents that never touch the same file. Nothing runs until you approve. Briefs, plans, and reports export as Markdown plus schema.org JSON-LD, so a directory of missions becomes a queryable history. ([Cloudscockpit/voltron-lions-claudecode](https://github.com/Cloudscockpit/voltron-lions-claudecode))
- **voluum-cli-assistant**: Community plugin with setup/install and command composition skills for voluum-cli. ([markab21/voluum-cli](https://github.com/markab21/voluum-cli/tree/HEAD/plugins/voluum-cli-assistant))
- **volverjs-auth-vue**: OAuth2 / OpenID Connect authentication for Vue 3 with @volverjs/auth-vue ([source](.))
- **vord-guardrail**: Gates every Edit/Write in this session against vord's Agent Permission Policy before the bytes reach disk. ([pmaojo/vord](https://github.com/pmaojo/vord/tree/HEAD/integrations/claude-code-plugin))
- **vornik-companion**: Async-offload companion. Delegate reviews, audits, validations, research, summarization, and bulk RAG ingest to vornik-managed agents over MCP; results re-enter Claude's context via a SessionStart digest hook. Per-repo scoping (migration 75) so one project's RAG cleanly serves multiple repos. Also bundles operator lifecycle skills — configure-vornik, validate-install, troubleshoot-vornik, and report-problem — so your assistant drives Vornik's own tooling (config validate/reload, the published reference architecture, the doctor, the failure-class playbook, and the anonymized problem reporter)… ([grinco/vornik](https://github.com/grinco/vornik/tree/HEAD/contrib/claude-code-companion))
- **vortex-tuck-in-analyst**: Calibrated, dual-mode tuck-in acquisition analysis for commercial door services and adjacent lower-middle-market field-service businesses. FULL mode runs a five-stage, review-gated pipeline through a screening memo; RECON mode (just a website or a name) produces a recon brief with an input-readiness diagnostic, stopping before earnings work. A Step 0 calibration confirms the run plan before dispatching agents. See the plugin README for full detail. ([ZaBrisket/vortex-tuck-in-analyst](https://github.com/ZaBrisket/vortex-tuck-in-analyst/tree/HEAD/plugins/vortex-tuck-in-analyst))
- **vosslab-skills**: Reusable workflow skills for software planning, code review, repository maintenance, documentation, engineering, and education content production. ([vosslab/vosslab-skills](https://github.com/vosslab/vosslab-skills))
- **vote-sim**: Group decision simulator — normal-distribution temperature controls N independent LLM agents to simulate human crowd opinion on any question ([quantumiodb/quantwise](https://github.com/quantumiodb/quantwise/tree/HEAD/plugins/vote-sim))
- **vouch**: Skills for running vouch with Claude Code or Codex: evidence-first setup, read-only version status, verified release updates, and safe program recognition. ([polston/vouch](https://github.com/polston/vouch/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **vouch**: Receipts, not recall: claim guard, grounding lock, loop monitor, per-model bankroll and autonomy tiers. Zero model calls on the happy path. ([Ravencloned/skills-for-claude](https://github.com/Ravencloned/skills-for-claude/tree/HEAD/vouch))
- **vouch**: Passive anti-hallucination hooks for Claude Code: auto-capture tool results as evidence, a deterministic commit gate, and an LLM reviewer that catches fabrication, ungrounded conclusions, skipped research, and unbacked external claims. The hooks call the globally-installed `vouch` CLI. ([sunnyadn/vouch](https://github.com/sunnyadn/vouch/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **vouch-protocol**: Integrate the Vouch Protocol into your codebase: sign and verify agent actions, delegation chains, the post-quantum proof set, and revocation, across SDKs on every major platform. ([vouch-protocol/vouch](https://github.com/vouch-protocol/vouch/tree/HEAD/claude-skill))
- **vouchington-database**: Repository-neutral PostgreSQL schema and performance guidance. ([vouchington/vouchington-tooling](https://github.com/vouchington/vouchington-tooling/tree/HEAD/plugins/vouchington-database))
- **vouchington-testing**: Repository-neutral testing guidance for common runners and application stacks. ([vouchington/vouchington-tooling](https://github.com/vouchington/vouchington-tooling/tree/HEAD/plugins/vouchington-testing))
- **vouchington-workflow**: Repository-neutral implementation, dependency automation, publishing, planning, issue, retrospective, CI, and PR workflow skills. ([vouchington/vouchington-tooling](https://github.com/vouchington/vouchington-tooling/tree/HEAD/plugins/vouchington-workflow))
- **voussoir**: Model test campaigns as a 10-layer self-supporting hierarchy, attest them with trace lineage, and cross-validate independent instruments through a tamper-evident verdict interchange (verify / compare / signed receipt chain). Ships the voussoir-naming skill, a /voussoir command, a validator subagent, and an MCP server (verify / compare / record / synthesize). ([acidblock/voussoir](https://github.com/acidblock/voussoir/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **vovk**: Claude Code plugin for Vovk.ts projects ([finom/vovk](https://github.com/finom/vovk))
- **vow-of-silence**: Claude takes a Trappist vow of silence: zero conversational prose — work flows only through tool calls, diffs, code blocks, and bracketed stage directions. ([quadseven/brother-claudius](https://github.com/quadseven/brother-claudius))
- **vox**: Voice for your AI coding assistant: /unmute, /mute, /vibe, /recap — hear when tasks finish or need input ([punt-labs/vox](https://github.com/punt-labs/vox/tree/HEAD/plugin))
- **vox-actor-plugin**: vox-actorを利用するためのプラグイン ([canpok1/vox-actor](https://github.com/canpok1/vox-actor/tree/HEAD/plugins/vox-actor-plugin))
- **vox-ai**: vox.ai voice agent development plugin — MCP tools + best practice skills ([vox-public/vox-skills](https://github.com/vox-public/vox-skills))
- **vox-gateway**: vox-gateway AI 텔레포니 커넥터 — Claude가 실제 전화를 걸고(make_call) 통화를 조회한다. Remote MCP(OAuth 2.1), 설치 후 브라우저 로그인. ([hodoolabs/vox-gateway-plugin](https://github.com/hodoolabs/vox-gateway-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/vox-gateway))
- **voxalign**: Voice-attribution SRT reconciler: per-channel mics plus an edited SRT to an accurate speaker-attributed transcript. ([CR8-OS/voxalign](https://github.com/CR8-OS/voxalign/tree/HEAD/plugins/voxalign))
- **voxcaster**: Persistent interactive PTY sessions for AI agents — spawn background processes, send stdin, read output, and get hands-free exit notifications via Claude Code channels. ([LunarLaurus/voxcaster](https://github.com/LunarLaurus/voxcaster))
- **voxclaw**: Give your coding agent a voice. Speaks responses aloud through the VoxClaw macOS/iOS app with per-project voices, speech queue, and polite multi-device sync. ([malpern/VoxClaw](https://github.com/malpern/VoxClaw/tree/HEAD/plugins/voxclaw))
- **voxclaw**: Speak Claude Code updates aloud through the VoxClaw macOS app, CLI, or local HTTP listener. ([malpern/codex-marketplace](https://github.com/malpern/codex-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/voxclaw))
